Stack & surfaces\n\nTwo independently deployed TypeScript services. **`nextjs/`** is a Next.js 15.5 App Router app on Vercel (React 18, next-intl v4 with 40 locales, HeroUI/Tailwind, Drizzle ORM over `node-postgres`, Better Auth with a magic-link plugin plus a custom `license-key` sign-in plugin, tRPC v10 with superjson, Stripe v20 + a legacy Polar SDK path, Resend for email, Upstash Redis for rate limiting). It is the **system of record for money**: license keys (\"Account Keys\"), the `credit_grants` ledger, the `credit_balances` denormalized cache, and `stripe_processed_events` idempotency table all live here, exposed to the outside world through 23 REST route handlers plus one tRPC catch-all. **`hyperwhisper-cloud/`** is a Bun/Hono service on Fly.io that proxies audio to 11 STT providers and 7 LLM providers; it holds **no database** — it authenticates by POSTing the license key to `nextjs`'s `/api/license/validate`, caches the `{isValid, credits}` verdict in Upstash Redis for 1h (`license:<key>`), and charges usage by POSTing back to `/api/license/credits`. Trust boundaries: the Fly service trusts only the TCP peer address (`fdaa::/16` ⇒ off-edge ⇒ IP is `unknown`); the Next.js service trusts the Stripe signature, an `x-internal-secret` header, a Better Auth session cookie, and — critically — **the bare license key as a bearer credential** for all `/api/license/*` and `/api/account/*` endpoints. Shared mutable state: `inFlightDeductions: Set<Promise>` (cloud, drained on SIGTERM), the `_jwtClient`/`_inflight` Google token singletons, the ~1h in-memory model-list cache in `nextjs/lib/services/model-list.ts`, and the Redis license cache which both services write. Concurrency points: fire-and-forget `deductCredits()` after response flush, `costPromise.then(...)` after an SSE stream, the Deepgram WebSocket bridge, Stripe webhook re-deliveries, and `db.transaction` blocks in `db-layer.ts`.\n\n## Entry points\n\n**hyperwhisper-cloud (Hono, `src/index.ts`)**\n- `GET /health`, `GET /warmup` — unauthenticated, no input.\n- `POST /transcribe` (`src/routes/transcribe.ts`) — raw `audio/*` body; `X-STT-Provider`/`X-STT-Model`/`X-STT-Domain` headers; `account_key`|`license_key`, `language`, `initial_prompt`, `mode` query params read via `rawQuery()`. Buffers up to 2 GB into RAM.\n- `POST /post-process` (`src/routes/post-process.ts`) — JSON `{text ≤100k chars, prompt, account_key|license_key}`; `X-LLM-Provider`/model headers.\n- `POST /assistant` (`src/routes/assistant.ts`) — `multipart/form-data`: `account_key`, `messages` (JSON, ≤6 MB, may contain inline base64 images), `prompt`, `image` file (≤10 MB). Returns an SSE stream from Anthropic.\n- `GET /usage` (`src/routes/usage.ts`) — `account_key`|`license_key`|`identifier`, `force_refresh` query params.\n- `GET /ws/streaming-deepgram` (`src/routes/ws-streaming-deepgram.ts`) — WebSocket upgrade; `account_key`, `language`, `vocabulary` query params; binary audio frames proxied to `wss://api.deepgram.com`.\n- `SIGTERM`/`SIGINT` handler — drains `inFlightDeductions` for 4 s.\n- `hyperwhisper-cloud/scripts/smoke-test.ts` — operator-run, hits the live service.\n\n**nextjs — public REST (all unauthenticated unless noted)**\n- `POST /api/license/validate` + alias `/api/account/validate` — `{license_key, include_credits, device_id, device_name}`. IP rate-limited 30/min. Falls back to a live Polar API call and can **mint a license + 5000 credits** on a DB miss.\n- `POST /api/license/activate` + `/api/account/activate` — same shape, rate-limited, legacy.\n- `POST /api/license/deactivate` + `/api/account/deactivate` — stub, always 200.\n- `GET /api/license/credits` + `/api/account/credits` — `?license_key=`; returns balance. **No rate limit.**\n- `POST /api/license/credits` + `/api/account/credits` — `{license_key, amount, metadata}`; **atomically deducts `amount` credits. No rate limit, no auth beyond the key.** This is the sole write path used by the Fly service.\n- `POST /api/checkout/credits` — `{amount: 5..500, licenseKey?, email?}`; creates Stripe customers and Checkout Sessions. **No auth, no rate limit.**\n- `GET /api/checkout` — redirect to `/credits`.\n- `POST /api/webhooks/stripe` — Stripe signature verified; handles `checkout.session.completed`, `checkout.session.async_payment_succeeded`, `checkout.session.async_payment_failed`, `charge.refunded`.\n- `POST /api/webhooks/add-blog-post` — Outrank bearer token (+ optional HMAC); upserts `blog_posts` and calls `revalidatePath`.\n- `GET /api/download` — `?platform=&arch=`; fetches an appcast XML and builds a dynamic `RegExp` from `arch`.\n- `GET /api/config`, `GET /api/internal/models`, `GET /models` — cached public JSON; `/models` and `/api/internal/models` fan out to 6 paid provider list APIs on a cache miss.\n- `GET|POST /api/auth/[...all]` — Better Auth (magic link, `/sign-in/license-key` at 5/min).\n- `GET|POST /api/trpc/[trpc]` — `download.*` (public), `customer.*` (protected), `admin.*` (admin).\n- `GET /api/customer/profile` — Better Auth session required.\n- `GET /[locale]/user/auth/sign-out` — route handler.\n\n**nextjs — internal REST (`x-internal-secret` header, timing-safe)**\n- `POST /api/internal/grant-license` — mints a key + credits for an email.\n- `POST /api/internal/granted-emails` — bulk email dump.\n- `POST /api/internal/licenses-for-email` — keys + balances for an email.\n\n**nextjs — middleware & rendering**\n- `middleware.ts` — matcher `/((?!api|models(?:/|$)|_next|_vercel|docs|.*\\..*).*)`. Gates `/{locale}/user/customers` with a real `fetch('/api/auth/get-session')` + `role === \"admin\"`; gates all other `/{locale}/user/*` on **cookie presence only**; runs `next-intl` for everything else.\n- 22 `page.tsx` files under `app/[locale]/`, plus `sitemap.ts` / `robots.ts`.\n\n## Invariants (ranked)\n\n### I1: Every credit reservation taken at preflight is ≥ the total cost actually deducted for that request, across all retries, fallbacks and re-prompts.\nWhy it matters: the reservation is the only thing standing between a low-balance user and unbounded provider spend the business eats; under-reserving turns each request into a free-money bug at HyperWhisper's expense.\nSearch strategy: `grep -n \"ESTIMATED_POST_PROCESS_CREDITS\\|ESTIMATED_ASSISTANT_CREDITS\\|estimateCreditsForProviderFallbacks\\|minimumStreamingCredits\"` — then trace each. `post-process.ts` reserves a **flat `1.0`** yet can spend `retriesFor(provider)` primary attempts (up to 3 for groq) + a fallback provider's full retry budget + a *prompt-leakage retry on a third provider* whose cost is `costUsd += retryResponse.costUsd`. `assistant.ts` reserves `3.0 × countInlineImages()` but the multipart `image` file adds an image `countInlineImages` never counts, and output tokens are unbounded. In `transcribe.ts`, cross-check `FALLBACK_CHAINS` against the `rates` array built in `estimateCreditsForProviderFallbacks` — grep `costUsd +=`, `retriesFor(`, `callWithRetry(`.\n\n### I2: Every upstream call that has already cost money results in exactly one `deductCredits()`, on every exit path — including thrown-exception and stream-error paths — and never more than one.\nWhy it matters: a missed deduction is silent revenue loss at scale; a duplicated one double-charges a paying customer.\nSearch strategy: in `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/routes/`, grep `deductCredits(` (7 call sites) and `return c.json\\|return errorResponse\\|return new Response`; for each `return` that follows a successful provider call, confirm a deduction precedes it. Specific suspects: `assistant.ts:350` — `costPromise.then(...)` bills only on resolve, so a rejected `costPromise` (Anthropic stream aborted mid-flight after tokens were billed) drops the charge entirely; `ws-streaming-deepgram.ts` `endSession()` is guarded by `sessionEnded` but is reachable from `onClose`, `onError`, and the Deepgram `close` listener concurrently. Also grep `performDeduction` — any call site bypassing `deductCredits()` escapes `inFlightDeductions` and is lost on SIGTERM.\n\n### I3: A `license:<key>` Redis entry with `isValid:false` is written only for a *definitive* verdict (HTTP 4xx from the licensing API); transient failures (5xx, timeout, network, unparseable body) must leave the cache untouched.\nWhy it matters: `LICENSE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS = 3600` — one Vercel cold start or a malformed body during a deploy locks a paying customer out of transcription for a full hour across every Fly region.\nSearch strategy: `grep -rn \"cacheLicense(\" hyperwhisper-cloud/src`. `middleware/auth.ts:70-79` implements the rule correctly (explicit `if (response.status >= 500) return ... ; // not caching`). `routes/usage.ts:44-52` (`validateLicenseAndGetCredits`) does **not**: it computes `isValid = data.valid === true` after `response.json().catch(() => ({}))` and unconditionally calls `cacheLicense` — so a 500, an HTML error page, or an empty body all poison the cache with `isValid:false`. Verify every `cacheLicense` call is preceded by a status check.\n\n### I4: Every STT adapter that returns a non-empty transcript returns a strictly positive `durationSeconds` (falling back to `estimateSecondsFromBytes` when the upstream duration field is absent or zero).\nWhy it matters: `costUsd` is `durationSeconds × rate` for duration-billed providers, so a missing field means a real, provider-billed transcription is charged **0 credits** — a free-transcription oracle triggered by any upstream response-shape change.\nSearch strategy: `grep -ln \"estimateSecondsFromBytes\" hyperwhisper-cloud/src/providers/*.ts` and diff against the 11 STT adapters. Present in `assemblyai.ts`, `mistral.ts`, `soniox.ts`, `openai.ts`, and `google-chirp.ts` (via its own `estimateAudioSeconds`); **absent in `deepgram.ts`, `groq.ts`, `elevenlabs.ts`, `xai-stt.ts`, `azure-mai.ts`**. For each, read where `durationSeconds` is derived (`data.metadata.duration`, `data.duration`, last word `.end`, `data.durationMilliseconds`) and confirm a guard before `compute*TranscriptionCost(...)`.\n\n### I5: Every Stripe webhook handler that mutates licenses or credits dedupes on a stable Stripe id before mutating, and returns non-2xx (so Stripe retries) on any *transient* failure.\nWhy it matters: Stripe delivers at-least-once and fires both `checkout.session.completed` and `checkout.session.async_payment_succeeded` for the same purchase; a missing guard double-grants credits, and swallowing an error returns 200 and permanently loses the grant.\nSearch strategy: in `nextjs/lib/services/stripe-webhook.ts`, grep `grantCreditLot(\\|grantCreditsForStripeEvent(\\|refundCreditGrant(\\|updateAccountKey(`. `handleCreditPurchase` correctly routes through `grantCreditsForStripeEvent` (keyed on `stripe_processed_events.stripe_object_id`); `handleLicensePurchase:126` calls the **un-deduped `grantCreditLot({sourceType:\"license_bundle\", sourceId: session.id})`** and its only guard is a prior `findAccountByStripeSession` read — check whether `grantCreditLotInTransaction`'s `onConflictDoNothing` target actually covers `(sourceType, sourceId)`. Also `app/api/webhooks/stripe/route.ts:121-130`: `handleChargeRefunded` errors are caught and the route still returns `{received:true}` — a refund clawback lost to a transient DB blip is never retried.\n\n### I6: No credit amount, price, or quantity that determines what a user is charged or granted is taken from client-controlled input without server-side re-derivation or a hard bound.\nWhy it matters: this is the direct path to minting free credits or draining another key's wallet.\nSearch strategy: `grep -rn \"body.amount\\|input.amount\\|metadata.credit_amount\\|creditAmount\" nextjs/app/api nextjs/lib nextjs/server`. `POST /api/license/credits` accepts `amount` straight off the wire (bounded only by `validateCreditDeductionAmount`: finite, >0, ≤1,000,000, 2 dp) and deducts it — with the license key as the sole credential and no rate limit, so anyone holding a leaked key can zero a balance in one request. `session.metadata.credit_amount` is trusted in `handleCreditPurchase`; confirm nothing but `/api/checkout/credits` can write that metadata. Confirm `computeCreditPurchase()` is the only source of `creditCents`/`feeCents` and that `components/credits/CreditsPurchase.tsx:92` sends only `amount`, never a price.\n\n### I7: Every unauthenticated Next.js route handler whose work fans out to a paid third party or writes a durable object is rate-limited.\nWhy it matters: one cheap POST maps 1:1 to a Polar call, a Stripe customer/session creation, or a 6-provider model fan-out — free amplification and quota/cost burn against an open endpoint.\nSearch strategy: `grep -rLn \"RateLimiter\\|\\.limit(\" nextjs/app/api/**/route.ts` and compare against `nextjs/lib/rate-limit.ts` (only `downloadEmailRateLimiter` and `licenseValidateRateLimiter` exist). `/api/license/validate` and `/api/license/activate` are covered; `/api/license/credits` (GET **and** the deducting POST), `/api/checkout/credits` (creates real `stripe.customers` + Checkout Sessions), `/api/download` (outbound appcast fetch + dynamic RegExp), and `/api/internal/models` are not. For `/api/internal/models` and `/models`, verify the `s-maxage=3600` header plus `fetchAvailableModels`'s in-memory TTL actually bound the fan-out on a cache-busted or cold-lambda request.\n\n### I8: Any handler returning or mutating a specific user's data derives that user from the Better Auth session (or an admin check), never from a client-supplied id; middleware cookie-presence is never the only gate.\nWhy it matters: `middleware.ts` checks only that `better-auth.session_token` **exists** for `/{locale}/user/*` — a forged or expired cookie value passes it. If a page or procedure then trusts that, an unauthenticated caller reads customer data.\nSearch strategy: for each of `app/[locale]/user/(authenticated)/{dashboard,devices,customers}/page.tsx` and `layout.tsx`, grep for `auth.api.getSession`; any page that renders account data without one relies on the presence-only check. Cross-check the tRPC routers: `grep -n \"publicProcedure\\|protectedProcedure\\|adminProcedure\" nextjs/server/api/routers/**/*.ts` — every `admin/*` procedure must use `adminProcedure` (`ctx.isAdmin` from `user.role === \"admin\"`), and every `customer.*` procedure must key off `ctx.user.id`/`ctx.user.email`, never an `input`-supplied id. Also verify `src/db/schema/auth.ts` marks `role` as `input: false` so it cannot be set at sign-up.\n\n### I9: Every comparison of a caller-supplied credential against an env secret is timing-safe **and** fails closed when the env var is unset or empty.\nWhy it matters: `timingSafeEqualSecret(received, expected)` returns `false` when `expected` is falsy — good — but any `===` comparison, or a route that skips the check when the env var is missing, silently opens an admin/mint endpoint in a misconfigured environment.\nSearch strategy: `grep -rn \"process.env\\..*SECRET\\|process.env\\..*TOKEN\\|process.env\\..*KEY\" nextjs/app/api nextjs/lib | grep -n \"===\\|!==\\|==\"`. Confirm all three `/api/internal/*` routes call `timingSafeEqualSecret(secret, process.env.HYPERWHISPER_INTERNAL_SECRET)` before any DB work (they do at line ~11 of each). In `app/api/webhooks/add-blog-post/route.ts`, check the interaction between `OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_SIGNING_SECRET` and `OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_TOKEN`: when signing is enabled, verify the bearer path cannot be used as an alternative bypass, and that a missing `OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_TOKEN` doesn't make `timingSafeEqualSecret` the only barrier to a mint-shaped mutation. Also `app/api/webhooks/stripe/route.ts:33-40` correctly 500s on a missing `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET` — confirm no sibling handler does the opposite.\n\n### I10: Every `fetch()` to a third-party API carries an `AbortSignal.timeout` or an `AbortController`.\nWhy it matters: a hung upstream leaves an unresolved promise pinning the request scope — on a 1 GB Fly machine that bloats the Bun worker until OOM-kill, and on Vercel it burns the full function max-duration.\nSearch strategy: `grep -rn \"fetch(\" hyperwhisper-cloud/src nextjs/lib nextjs/app | grep -v \"signal:\"`. In the cloud service all 10 STT adapters route through `fetchWithTimeout()` in `providers/utils.ts` (default `DEFAULT_PROVIDER_TIMEOUT_MS = 15_000`), but **all 7 LLM provider files — `anthropic.ts`, `cerebras.ts`, `groq-llm.ts`, `gemini-llm.ts`, `mistral-llm.ts`, `openai-llm.ts`, `xai-llm.ts` — use bare `fetch()` with no timeout** (`streamAnthropicChat`'s AbortController fires only on client disconnect, not on a stall). Note `gcs-storage.ts` maintains a *second, duplicated* `fetchWithTimeoutMs` implementation that can drift from `providers/utils.ts`. On the Next.js side check `lib/services/model-list.ts` `apiGet()` and the Polar SDK calls in `src/lib/license-validation.ts` (no timeout is configurable there, yet it sits behind an unauthenticated endpoint).\n\n### I11: In the WebSocket bridge, every acquired resource is released on every path, and closing one side always closes the other.\nWhy it matters: an orphaned upstream Deepgram socket keeps metering billable audio and holds a Fly file descriptor; a leaked `setInterval` survives the request.\nSearch strategy: read `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/routes/ws-streaming-deepgram.ts` end to end and check each early `return`. The `MAX_SESSION_AUDIO_BYTES` breach at line 308 sends an error and calls `clientSocket.close(1009)` but **never calls `closeUpstream()` / `deepgramWs.close()`** — verify whether the resulting client close reliably fires `onClose`. Also: `pingInterval` is cleared only inside `endSession()`; the `apiKey`-missing early return at line 238 sets it never, but confirm no path sets the interval then exits without `endSession()`. Grep `setInterval(\\|clearInterval(\\|getReader()\\|releaseLock()\\|\\.cancel(` across `src/` — `providers/anthropic.ts`'s `streamAnthropicChat` `start()` catch block never calls `reader.cancel()` when the failure is not a client abort.\n\n### I12: The provider and model reported back to the client (`X-STT-Provider`, `X-STT-Model`, deduction `metadata`) always names the provider/model that actually ran and was priced.\nWhy it matters: `transcribe.ts:669` does `result.source as Provider` and looks the price up from that; if an adapter returns a `source` that isn't its own registry id, or reports a `model` it didn't run, the charge is computed against the wrong rate table and support has no audit trail.\nSearch strategy: `grep -n \"source:\" hyperwhisper-cloud/src/providers/*.ts` and check each returned literal against `TranscriptionSource` in `providers/types.ts` **and** against `SttProviderId` in `lib/stt-models.ts` — these are two parallel unions that must stay in sync (`TranscriptionSource` additionally carries `'no_speech'`). Then confirm `usedModel = result.model || attemptModel` (line 511) is what reaches both the `X-STT-Model` header and `stt_model` in the `deductCredits` metadata, and that `getProviderDef(current).defaultModel` on a fallback hop matches the rate actually used in `estimateCreditsForProviderFallbacks`. Also check `cost-calculator.ts`'s `rates[model] ?? rates[defaultModel]` fallback — an unknown model id is silently re-priced at the default rate.\n\n### I13: Every value crossing a trust boundary is validated (or allow-listed/escaped) before it is used to index, interpolate, allocate, or render.\nWhy it matters: these are the crash-and-injection sinks — an unvalidated `req.json()` destructure 500s, an unescaped name lands script in a transactional email from a trusted sender, an unbounded `Content-Length` allocates memory before auth.\nSearch strategy: (a) `grep -rn \"await req.json()\\|await request.json()\\|c.req.json()\" nextjs/app/api hyperwhisper-cloud/src` and confirm each result is shape-checked before destructuring — `app/api/license/credits/route.ts:82` does `const body = await req.json(); const { license_key, amount, metadata } = body;` with no null guard and an entirely unvalidated `metadata` that is then logged. (b) `grep -rn \"escapeHtml\" nextjs/lib/templates/` — all four templates escape `customerName`; verify every *other* interpolated field in the 17/15/21/14 `${...}` sites is either a number or system-generated. (c) `grep -rn \"dangerouslySetInnerHTML\" nextjs` — `app/[locale]/blog/[slug]/page.tsx:170` renders `post.html` raw from the Outrank webhook. (d) `grep -rn \"new RegExp(\" nextjs` — `app/api/download/route.ts:66` builds a RegExp from `arch`, guarded by `parseWindowsArch`'s allow-list; confirm no sibling does the same without one. (e) Confirm `Content-Length` is parsed and bounded *before* `arrayBuffer()`/`formData()` on every cloud route.\n\n### I14: No user-facing path hardcodes a single locale, and every message key referenced by a component exists in all 40 locale files.\nWhy it matters: `localePrefix: \"always\"` over 40 locales means a hardcoded `/en/...` silently strips 39 languages from a cache invalidation or a post-auth redirect, and a missing key makes `next-intl` throw at render — a hard 500 on that locale only, invisible from an English-language smoke test.\nSearch strategy: `grep -rn '\"/en/\\|`/en/\\|/en/' nextjs/app nextjs/src nextjs/lib` — `app/api/webhooks/add-blog-post/route.ts:386,403` calls `revalidatePath(\"/en/blog/...\")` and `revalidatePath(\"/en/blog\")` only, so 39 locales keep serving stale blog indexes; `src/lib/license-key-redirect.ts:1` pins `DEFAULT_LICENSE_KEY_REDIRECT = \"/en/user/dashboard\"`. {\n        isValid: true,\n        credits: data.credits_remaining,\n        cachedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n      });\n    }\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.warn('POST /api/license/credits network error', {\n      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n    });\n  }\n}\n\n// In-flight deduction tracking for graceful shutdown.\n// Call sites fire deductCredits() without awaiting (response latency), so a\n// Fly machine recycle (SIGTERM on deploy/scale-down) between the response\n// flush and the redis/license write would silently drop the charge. Every\n// deduction registers here so the SIGTERM handler can drain before exit.\nconst inFlightDeductions = new Set<Promise<number>>();\n\nexport async function drainPendingDeductions(timeoutMs: number): Promise<number> {\n  const pendingCount = inFlightDeductions.size;\n  if (pendingCount === 0) {\n    return 0;\n  }\n\n  const allSettled = Promise.allSettled([...inFlightDeductions]);\n  const timeout = new Promise<void>((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, timeoutMs));\n  await Promise.race([allSettled, timeout]);\n  return pendingCount;\n}\n\nexport function deductCredits(\n  auth: AuthContext,\n  costUsd: number,\n  metadata: Record<string, unknown>,\n  clientIP: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const deduction = performDeduction(auth, costUsd, metadata, clientIP);\n  inFlightDeductions.add(deduction);\n  deduction\n    .catch(() => {}) // errors are logged inside performDeduction / by callers\n    .finally(() => inFlightDeductions.delete(deduction));\n  return deduction;\n}\n\nasync function performDeduction(\n  auth: AuthContext,\n  costUsd: number,\n  metadata: Record<string, unknown>,\n  _clientIP: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const creditsUsed = creditsForCost(costUsd);\n\n  if (creditsUsed <= 0) {\n    return 0;\n  }\n\n  await recordLicenseUsage(auth.identifier, creditsUsed, metadata);\n  return creditsUsed;\n}\n","numLines":147,"startLine":1,"totalLines":147}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9eab2e3c-c41d-42bf-9a2f-11b6ffa82254","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

At low sample rates (≤16 kHz) the AAC encoder\n        // clamps to its own ceiling regardless of what we ask for, so request a value the\n        // encoder won't have to renegotiate — 32 kbps mono / 64 kbps stereo are safe defaults\n        // for telephony-grade speech and match what Core Audio picks internally.\n        let bitrate: Int\n        if channels <= 1 {\n            bitrate = 32_000\n        } else if channels == 2 {\n            bitrate = 64_000\n        } else {\n            bitrate = max(32_000 * channels, 64_000)\n        }\n\n        // STEP 8: Configure AAC encoder settings.\n        // AVEncoderAudioQualityKey is omitted on purpose: when AVEncoderBitRateKey is set,\n        // mixing the two produced \"Cannot Encode Media\" on the first append on macOS 26.\n        let outputSettings: [String: Any] = [\n            AVFormatIDKey: kAudioFormatMPEG4AAC,\n            AVSampleRateKey: sampleRate,\n            AVNumberOfChannelsKey: channels,\n            AVEncoderBitRateKey: bitrate\n        ]\n\n        let writerInput = AVAssetWriterInput(mediaType: .audio, outputSettings: outputSettings)\n        writerInput.expectsMediaDataInRealTime = false  // We're converting a file, not streaming\n\n        guard assetWriter.canAdd(writerInput) else {\n            AppLogger.audio.error(\"convertAudioToAAC: writer.canAdd(input) returned false for AAC \\(Int(sampleRate))Hz \\(channels)ch @ \\(bitrate)bps; writerError=\\(assetWriter.error?.localizedDescription ?? Log the full error\n// server-side with an error_id the client can quote to support.\napp.onError((err, c) => {\n  const errorId = crypto.randomUUID();\n  console.error(`Unhandled error [${errorId}]:`, err);\n  return c.json({\n    error: 'Internal server error',\n    message: 'An unexpected error occurred. Please try again.',\n    error_id: errorId,\n  }, 500);\n});\n\n// Graceful shutdown: Fly sends SIGTERM on deploy/scale-down with a grace\n// period before SIGKILL (kill_timeout, default 5s). Credit deductions are\n// fired without awaiting after the response is flushed, so drain any that are\n// still in flight before exiting — otherwise the user keeps the transcript\n// but the charge is silently dropped.\nconst SHUTDOWN_DRAIN_MS = 4_000;\nlet shuttingDown = false;\n\nasync function gracefulShutdown(signal: string): Promise<void> {\n  if (shuttingDown) return;\n  shuttingDown = true;\n\n  console.log('machine.shutdown', {\n    signal,\n    machineId: process.env.FLY_MACHINE_ID || 'local',\n    shutdownAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n  });\n\n  const drained = await drainPendingDeductions(SHUTDOWN_DRAIN_MS);\n  if (drained > 0) {\n    console.log('machine.shutdown_drained_deductions', { count: drained });\n  }\n  process.exit(0);\n}\n\nprocess.on('SIGTERM', () => { void gracefulShutdown('SIGTERM'); });\nprocess.on('SIGINT', () => { void gracefulShutdown('SIGINT'); });\n\n// Export for Bun\nexport default {\n  port: Number(process.env.PORT) || 8080,\n  fetch: app.fetch,\n  websocket,\n};\n\nconsole.log('machine.boot', {\n  region: process.env.FLY_REGION || 'local',\n  machineId: process.env.FLY_MACHINE_ID || 'local',\n  allocId: process.env.FLY_ALLOC_ID || undefined,\n  imageRef: process.env.FLY_IMAGE_REF || undefined,\n  port: Number(process.env.PORT) || 8080,\n  bootAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n});\nconsole.log(`HyperWhisper Fly.io service starting on port ${process.env.PORT || 8080}`);","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"c80b08f6-a7c5-4cfa-b204-9714decf3d91","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:47:04.661Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"app.notFound((c) => {\n  return c.text('Method not allowed', 405);\n});\n\n// Error handler — never echo raw err.message to the client (it can contain\n// env-var names, upstream provider bodies, request IDs). Credit deductions are\n// fired without awaiting after the response is flushed, so drain any that are\n// still in flight before exiting — otherwise the user keeps the transcript\n// but the charge is silently dropped.\nconst SHUTDOWN_DRAIN_MS = 4_000;\nlet shuttingDown = false;\n\nasync function gracefulShutdown(signal: string): Promise<void> {\n  if (shuttingDown) return;\n  shuttingDown = true;\n\n  console.log('machine.shutdown', {\n    signal,\n    machineId: process.env.FLY_MACHINE_ID || 'local',\n    shutdownAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n  });\n\n  const drained = await drainPendingDeductions(SHUTDOWN_DRAIN_MS);\n  if (drained > 0) {\n    console.log('machine.shutdown_drained_deductions', { count: drained });\n  }\n  process.exit(0);\n}\n\nprocess.on('SIGTERM', () => { void gracefulShutdown('SIGTERM'); });\nprocess.on('SIGINT', () => { void gracefulShutdown('SIGINT'); });\n\n// Export for Bun\nexport default {\n  port: Number(process.env.PORT) || 8080,\n  fetch: app.fetch,\n  websocket,\n};\n\nconsole.log('machine.boot', {\n  region: process.env.FLY_REGION || 'local',\n  machineId: process.env.FLY_MACHINE_ID || 'local',\n  allocId: process.env.FLY_ALLOC_ID || undefined,\n  imageRef: process.env.FLY_IMAGE_REF || undefined,\n  port: Number(process.env.PORT) || 8080,\n  bootAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n});\nconsole.log(`HyperWhisper Fly.io service starting on port ${process.env.PORT || 8080}`);","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"701eaf67-c222-4141-96dd-e300d8db1de8","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"7b4bdee1-a9af-48b4-ba8b-bc815034d754","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"872714b7-1488-4cc4-8bad-5341f8c86fd3","agentId":"a90e691c4f3fd6396","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01T55dh7aKFdKL39vLNLwEZu","type":"tool_result","content":"  soniox: transcribeWithSoniox,\n};\n\n/**\n * Preflight credit reservation. For the primary provider we estimate against\n * the chosen model (and medical add-on); for fallback siblings we estimate\n * against their default model. The reservation uses the most expensive member\n * of the chain so we never under-reserve. `model`/`medical` are optional to\n * keep the historical 2-arg call signature working.\n */\nexport function estimateCreditsForProviderFallbacks(\n  sizeBytes: number,\n  provider: Provider,\n  model?: string,\n  medical: boolean = false,\n  initialPrompt?: string,\n  language?: string,\n): number {\n  const chain = FALLBACK_CHAINS[provider];\n  const estimatedSeconds = estimateAudioSecondsFromSize(sizeBytes);\n  const hasInitialPrompt = Boolean(initialPrompt);\n  const rates = chain.map((p) => estimatedUsdPerMinute(\n    p,\n    p === provider ? model : undefined,\n    p === provider ? medical : false,\n    // The keyterm surcharge is billed by ANY chain member that supports it\n    // (ElevenLabs scribe_v2 / AssemblyAI universal-3-pro) whenever an\n    // initial_prompt is present — not just the primary provider. Expired\n      -- grants are excluded above and never spent.\n      ORDER BY\n        expires_at ASC,\n        created_at ASC,\n        id\n      FOR UPDATE\n    `);\n\n    let remainingToDeduct = amount;\n    let deductedAmount = 0;\n\n    for (let index = 0; index < result.rows.length; index++) {\n      if (remainingToDeduct <= 0) break;\n\n      const grant = result.rows[index];\n      const grantRemaining = Number(grant.remaining_amount);\n      const deduction = Math.min(grantRemaining, remainingToDeduct);\n\n      if (deduction <= 0) continue;\n\n      const newRemaining = grantRemaining - deduction;\n      await tx\n        .update(creditGrants)\n        .set({\n          remainingAmount: newRemaining.toString(),\n          status: newRemaining === 0 ? \"spent\" : \"active\",\n          updatedAt: new Date(),\n        })\n        .where(eq(creditGrants.id, grant.id));\n\n      remainingToDeduct -= deduction;\n      deductedAmount += deduction;\n    }\n\n    // Reconcile the cached balance to the authoritative grant total in the same\n    // transaction (and same locked view). This both (a) reads via `tx` instead\n    // of a second pooled connection, and (b) self-heals any drift between the\n    // credit_balances cache and the grant rows, so the cache can never report\n    // spendable credits the grants can't actually back, or vice versa.\n    const balance = await reconcileCreditBalance(tx, userId);\n\n    return { balance, deductedAmount };\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Atomically deduct credits from source-tracked grants, flooring at zero.\n *\n * Returns the new cached aggregate balance. Primary method: AudioFileConverter.convertAudioToAAC()\n    ///    - Uses AVAssetReader/Writer pipeline\n    ///    - Preserves audio quality with 64-128 kbps AAC\n    ///\n    /// 2. Fallback method: AudioFileConverter.convertAudioToM4AWithExportSession()\n    ///    - Uses higher-level AVAssetExportSession API\n    ///    - Better compatibility for edge cases\n    ///\n    /// - Parameters:\n    ///   - wavURL: URL of the WAV file to potentially convert\n    ///   - context: Logging context string\n    ///\n    /// - Returns: M4A URL if conversion succeeded, original WAV URL otherwise\n    private func convertTrimmedToM4AIfNeeded(_ wavURL: URL, context: String) async -> URL {\n        let logPrefix = context.isEmpty ? \"\" : \"[\\(context)] \"\n\n        // CHECK: Only process WAV files\n=== 670,700 RecordingLifecycle ===\n                            data: [\n                                \"wavPath\": finalWavURL.path,\n                                \"wavSizeBytes\": wavSize,\n                                \"sampleRate\": 16000,\n                                \"channels\": 1\n                            ]\n                        )\n                    }\n                } else {\n                    // WAV is too large - must convert to M4A for upload\n                    // RELIABILITY FIX: Use two-stage fallback to prevent data loss\n                    // Stage 1: Try AVAssetReader/Writer (primary, more control)\n                    // Stage 2: Try AVAssetExportSession (fallback, different code path)\n                    // Stage 3: Keep WAV as-is (last resort, file may be too large for some APIs)\n                    AppLogger.audio.info(\"WAV file size (\\(wavSize / (1024 * 1024))MB) exceeds 25MB - converting to M4A\")\n\n                    let m4aURL = dstURL.deletingPathExtension().appendingPathExtension(\"m4a\")\n                    let m4aResult = await performM4AConversionWithRetries(from: rawURL, to: m4aURL)\n\n                    if m4aResult.success {\n                        self.finalURL = m4aURL\n                        // Delete WAV since M4A was created successfully\n                        try? Primary method: AudioFileConverter.convertAudioToAAC()\n    ///    - Uses AVAssetReader/Writer pipeline\n    ///    - Preserves audio quality with 64-128 kbps AAC\n    ///\n    /// 2. {"parentUuid":"8c288c6f-138b-408d-ae96-98da31e32a60","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"451045cb-1115-4894-87ea-8325dd8c8f72","agentId":"a90e691c4f3fd6396","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FbSgGVPK1n5hAoCyYFn9Pa","type":"tool_result","content":"25:export function roundToTenth(value: number): number {\n26-  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {\n27-    return 0;\n28-  }\n29-\n30-  const rounded = Math.round((value + Number.EPSILON) * 10) / 10;\n31-  return Math.abs(rounded) < Number.EPSILON ? 0 : rounded;\n32-}\n33-\n--\n37:export function roundUpToTenth(value: number): number {\n38-  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {\n39-    return 0;\n40-  }\n41-\n42-  const rounded = Math.ceil((value - Number.EPSILON) * 10) / 10;\n43-  return rounded <= 0 ? 0 : rounded;\n44-}\n45-\n=== validator ===\nexport const MAX_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_AMOUNT = 1_000_000;\n\n// credit_balances.balance is numeric(_, 2); amounts with finer precision than\n// the column scale would be silently rounded by Postgres on write, so a\n// sub-cent deduction (e.g. 0.005) can net a zero deduction. Reject anything\n// that does not round-trip at 2 decimal places.\nexport const CREDIT_BALANCE_SCALE = 2;\n\nexport function validateCreditDeductionAmount(amount: unknown): string | null {\n  if (typeof amount !== \"number\" || !Number.isFinite(amount) || amount <= 0) {\n    return \"amount must be a finite positive number\";\n  }\n\n  if (amount > MAX_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_AMOUNT) {\n    return `amount must be ${MAX_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_AMOUNT} or less`;\n  }\n\n  if (Number(amount.toFixed(CREDIT_BALANCE_SCALE)) !== amount) {\n    return `amount must have at most ${CREDIT_BALANCE_SCALE} decimal places`;\n  }\n\n  return null;\n}","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"45df406e-6a04-4f6c-b0b8-3578d3dc2be6","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:48:55.262Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"25:export function roundToTenth(value: number): number {\n26-  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {\n27-    return 0;\n28-  }\n29-\n30-  const rounded = Math.round((value + Number.EPSILON) * 10) / 10;\n31-  return Math.abs(rounded) < Number.EPSILON ? 0 : rounded;\n32-}\n33-\n--\n37:export function roundUpToTenth(value: number): number {\n38-  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {\n39-    return 0;\n40-  }\n41-\n42-  const rounded = Math.ceil((value - Number.EPSILON) * 10) / 10;\n43-  return rounded <= 0 ? 0 : rounded;\n44-}\n45-\n=== validator ===\nexport const MAX_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_AMOUNT = 1_000_000;\n\n// credit_balances.balance is numeric(_, 2); amounts with finer precision than\n// the column scale would be silently rounded by Postgres on write, so a\n// sub-cent deduction (e.g. 0.005) can net a zero deduction. Reject anything\n// that does not round-trip at 2 decimal places.\nexport const CREDIT_BALANCE_SCALE = 2;\n\nexport function validateCreditDeductionAmount(amount: unknown): string | null {\n  if (typeof amount !== \"number\" || !Number.isFinite(amount) || amount <= 0) {\n    return \"amount must be a finite positive number\";\n  }\n\n  if (amount > MAX_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_AMOUNT) {\n    return `amount must be ${MAX_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_AMOUNT} or less`;\n  }\n\n  if (Number(amount.toFixed(CREDIT_BALANCE_SCALE)) !== amount) {\n    return `amount must have at most ${CREDIT_BALANCE_SCALE} decimal places`;\n  }\n\n  return null;\n}","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"8c288c6f-138b-408d-ae96-98da31e32a60","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"4555f6a2-bf13-4a4a-9eee-ee38d3cfdd2a","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d3877233-d111-4961-bc3e-0b2f869ea728","agentId":"a90e691c4f3fd6396","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FLaSwY6YCKv8R8BE5KGew3","type":"tool_result","content":"import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\n\nimport {\n  upsertDeviceValidation,\n  getCreditBalance,\n} from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\nimport { checkLicenseKey } from \"@/src/lib/license-validation\";\nimport { licenseValidateRateLimiter } from \"@/lib/rate-limit\";\nimport { getClientIPFromHeaders } from \"@/server/api/routers/download-ip\";\n\n/**\n * License Validation API\n *\n * Validates license keys against the database.\n * Supports both Polar-issued and Stripe-issued licenses.\n *\n * DEVICE TRACKING:\n * When device_id is provided, tracks the device validation in the\n * device_validations table for fair usage policy monitoring.\n *\n * BACKWARD COMPATIBILITY:\n * - macOS app calls this endpoint with same request/response format\n * - device_id is optional - old apps without it still work\n *\n * EXTENDED RESPONSE (when include_credits=true):\n * - Returns credit balance for CF Workers to cache\n * - Used by HyperWhisper Cloud for usage-based billing\n *\n * CHECKS:\n * 1. License key exists in database\n * 2. Status is \"granted\" (not revoked/disabled)\n *\n * POLAR FALLBACK:\n * If license not found in database, validates against Polar API.\n * If valid via Polar, imports the license to the database with 5000 credits.\n * (Lookup + fallback + status check live in src/lib/license-validation.ts,\n * shared with the legacy /activate endpoint.)\n */\n\n/**\n * Tracks device validation for fair usage monitoring.\n * This is non-blocking - errors are logged but don't fail the validation.\n */\nasync function trackDeviceValidation(\n  licenseKeyId: string,\n  deviceId: string,\n  deviceName?: string\n): Promise<void> {\n  try {\n    await upsertDeviceValidation(licenseKeyId, deviceId, deviceName);\n  } catch (error) {\n    // Log but don't fail validation - tracking is non-critical\n    console.error(\"Device tracking error:\", error);\n  }\n}\n\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  // Rate limit by IP before any DB lookup or Polar fallback. The fallback\n  // issues a live outbound Polar request on every unknown key, so this caps\n  // the amplification an unauthenticated caller can drive.\n  const clientIP = getClientIPFromHeaders(req.headers);\n  const { success } = await licenseValidateRateLimiter.limit(clientIP);\n\n  if (!success) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { valid: false, error: \"Too many requests. Please try again later.\" },\n      { status: 429 }\n    );\n  }\n\n  let body: unknown;\n  try {\n    body = await req.json();\n  } catch {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { valid: false, error: \"Invalid request body\" },\n      { status: 400 }\n    );\n  }\n\n  const { license_key, include_credits, device_id, device_name } =\n    (body ?? {}) as {\n      license_key?: string;\n      include_credits?: boolean;\n      device_id?: string;\n      device_name?: string;\n    };\n\n  if (!license_key) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { valid: false, error: \"License key is required\" },\n      { status: 400 }\n    );\n  }\n\n  try {\n    // Lookup in database, with Polar fallback + status check\n    const result = await checkLicenseKey(license_key);\n\n    if (!result.valid) {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { valid: false, error: result.error },\n        { status: result.status }\n      );\n    }\n\n    const license = result.license;\n\n    // DEVICE TRACKING: Record device validation for fair usage monitoring\n    if (device_id) {\n      await trackDeviceValidation(license.id, device_id, device_name);\n    }\n\n    // License is valid - return extended info if requested\n    if (include_credits) {\n      const credits = await getCreditBalance(license.userId);\n\n      return NextResponse.json({\n        valid: true,\n        credits,\n        stripe_customer_id: license.stripeCustomerId || null,\n      });\n    }\n\n    // Basic response for macOS app compatibility\n    return NextResponse.json({ valid: true });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(\"License validation error:\", error);\n\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { valid: false, error: \"Failed to validate license. Please try again later.\" },\n      { status: 500 }\n    );\n  }\n}","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"30678c8f-3e88-4dd6-b7b0-ffc2177f2b12","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:49:12.250Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\n\nimport {\n  upsertDeviceValidation,\n  getCreditBalance,\n} from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\nimport { checkLicenseKey } from \"@/src/lib/license-validation\";\nimport { licenseValidateRateLimiter } from \"@/lib/rate-limit\";\nimport { getClientIPFromHeaders } from \"@/server/api/routers/download-ip\";\n\n/**\n * License Validation API\n *\n * Validates license keys against the database.\n * Supports both Polar-issued and Stripe-issued licenses.\n *\n * DEVICE TRACKING:\n * When device_id is provided, tracks the device validation in the\n * device_validations table for fair usage policy monitoring.\n *\n * BACKWARD COMPATIBILITY:\n * - macOS app calls this endpoint with same request/response format\n * - device_id is optional - old apps without it still work\n *\n * EXTENDED RESPONSE (when include_credits=true):\n * - Returns credit balance for CF Workers to cache\n * - Used by HyperWhisper Cloud for usage-based billing\n *\n * CHECKS:\n * 1. License key exists in database\n * 2. Status is \"granted\" (not revoked/disabled)\n *\n * POLAR FALLBACK:\n * If license not found in database, validates against Polar API.\n * If valid via Polar, imports the license to the database with 5000 credits.\n * (Lookup + fallback + status check live in src/lib/license-validation.ts,\n * shared with the legacy /activate endpoint.)\n */\n\n/**\n * Tracks device validation for fair usage monitoring.\n * This is non-blocking - errors are logged but don't fail the validation.\n */\nasync function trackDeviceValidation(\n  licenseKeyId: string,\n  deviceId: string,\n  deviceName?: string\n): Promise<void> {\n  try {\n    await upsertDeviceValidation(licenseKeyId, deviceId, deviceName);\n  } catch (error) {\n    // Log but don't fail validation - tracking is non-critical\n    console.error(\"Device tracking error:\", error);\n  }\n}\n\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  // Rate limit by IP before any DB lookup or Polar fallback. The fallback\n  // issues a live outbound Polar request on every unknown key, so this caps\n  // the amplification an unauthenticated caller can drive.\n  const clientIP = getClientIPFromHeaders(req.headers);\n  const { success } = await licenseValidateRateLimiter.limit(clientIP);\n\n  if (!success) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { valid: false, error: \"Too many requests. Please try again later.\" },\n      { status: 429 }\n    );\n  }\n\n  let body: unknown;\n  try {\n    body = await req.json();\n  } catch {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { valid: false, error: \"Invalid request body\" },\n      { status: 400 }\n    );\n  }\n\n  const { license_key, include_credits, device_id, device_name } =\n    (body ?? {}) as {\n      license_key?: string;\n      include_credits?: boolean;\n      device_id?: string;\n      device_name?: string;\n    };\n\n  if (!license_key) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { valid: false, error: \"License key is required\" },\n      { status: 400 }\n    );\n  }\n\n  try {\n    // Lookup in database, with Polar fallback + status check\n    const result = await checkLicenseKey(license_key);\n\n    if (!result.valid) {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { valid: false, error: result.error },\n        { status: result.status }\n      );\n    }\n\n    const license = result.license;\n\n    // DEVICE TRACKING: Record device validation for fair usage monitoring\n    if (device_id) {\n      await trackDeviceValidation(license.id, device_id, device_name);\n    }\n\n    // License is valid - return extended info if requested\n    if (include_credits) {\n      const credits = await getCreditBalance(license.userId);\n\n      return NextResponse.json({\n        valid: true,\n        credits,\n        stripe_customer_id: license.stripeCustomerId || null,\n      });\n    }\n\n    // Basic response for macOS app compatibility\n    return NextResponse.json({ valid: true });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(\"License validation error:\", error);\n\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { valid: false, error: \"Failed to validate license. drizzleAccountKeyToRow(row) : null;\n168-}\n169-\n170-export async function findAccountByStripeSession(sessionId: string): Promise<AccountKeyRow | null> {\n171-  const row = await db.query.accountKeys.findFirst({\n172-    where: eq(accountKeys.stripeSessionId, sessionId),\n173-  });\n174-  return row ? drizzleAccountKeyToRow(row) : null;\n175-}\n176-\n177-export async function findAccountByPolarLicenseKeyId(\n178-  polarLicenseKeyId: string\n179-): Promise<AccountKeyRow | null> {\n180-  const row = await db.query.accountKeys.findFirst({\n181-    where: eq(accountKeys.polarLicenseKeyId, polarLicenseKeyId),\n=== checkLicenseKey ===\nimport { polarClient, POLAR_ORGANIZATION_ID } from \"@/lib/clients/polar\";\nimport {\n  findAccountByKey,\n  findAccountById,\n  findAccountByPolarLicenseKeyId,\n  insertAccountKey,\n  grantCreditLot,\n  getOrCreateUser,\n  type AccountKeyRow,\n} from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\n\n/**\n * Shared license key validation logic.\n *\n * Used by both /api/license/validate and /api/license/activate so that\n * every entry point performs a real database check (with Polar fallback)\n * instead of trusting the key blindly.\n */\n\n/**\n * Import a license from Polar into the local database.\n *\n * Called when a license key is not found in the database but may exist in Polar.\n * Creates the license record, user (if needed), and grants 5000 credits.\n */\nasync function importLicenseFromPolar(licenseKey: string): Promise<{\n  success: boolean;\n  licenseId?: string;\n  error?: string;\n}> {\n  try {\n    // 1. Validate with Polar API\n    const polarResult = await polarClient.customerPortal.licenseKeys.validate({\n      key: licenseKey,\n      organizationId: POLAR_ORGANIZATION_ID,\n    });\n\n    // 2. Check if valid\n    if (polarResult.status !== \"granted\") {\n      return { success: false, error: `License is ${polarResult.status}` };\n    }\n\n    // 2b. Dedupe by the stable Polar license-key id (casing-independent).\n    // findAccountByKey is a case-sensitive exact match, so a different casing\n    // or whitespace variant of an already-imported key misses the DB lookup\n    // and reaches this fallback. Without this guard each variant would insert a\n    // fresh license row and grant another 5000 credits. polarResult.id is the\n    // canonical resource id regardless of how the input key was cased.\n    const alreadyImported = await findAccountByPolarLicenseKeyId(polarResult.id);\n    if (alreadyImported) {\n      return { success: true, licenseId: alreadyImported.id };\n    }\n\n    // 3. Get customer email - required for import\n    const email = polarResult.customer?.email;\n    if (!email) {\n      return { success: false, error: \"License has no associated email\" };\n    }\n\n    // 4. Get or create user\n    const user = await getOrCreateUser(email, {\n      name: polarResult.customer?.name ?? undefined,\n      polarCustomerId: polarResult.customerId ?? undefined,\n    });\n    if (!user) {\n      return { success: false, error: \"Failed to get or create user\" };\n    }\n\n    // 5. Insert license into database\n    const license = await insertAccountKey({\n      key: licenseKey,\n      email: email.toLowerCase().trim(),\n      userId: user.id,\n      polarLicenseKeyId: polarResult.id,\n      polarCustomerId: polarResult.customerId ?? null,\n      status: \"granted\",\n    });\n\n    if (!license) {\n      console.error(\"Failed to insert license from Polar\");\n      return { success: false, error: \"Failed to import license\" };\n    }\n\n    // 6. Create credit balance with 5000 credits\n    try {\n      await grantCreditLot({\n        userId: license.userId,\n        amount: 5000,\n        sourceType: \"polar_bundle\",\n        sourceId: polarResult.id,\n      });\n    } catch (creditError) {\n      console.error(\"Failed to create credit balance:\", creditError);\n      // License was created, but credits failed - still return success\n    }\n\n    console.log(\n      `Imported license from Polar: ${licenseKey} for ${email} with 5000 credits`\n    );\n\n    return { success: true, licenseId: license.id };\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error(\"Polar license import error:\", err);\n    return {\n      success: false,\n      error: \"Failed to validate with Polar\",\n    };\n  }\n}\n\nexport type LicenseCheckResult =\n  | { valid: true; license: AccountKeyRow }\n  | { valid: false; error: string; status: number };\n\n/**\n * Checks whether a license key is valid (exists and is \"granted\").\n *\n * 1. Looks up the key in the database\n * 2. POLAR FALLBACK: if not found, validates against Polar and imports it\n * 3. Verifies the license status is \"granted\" (not revoked/disabled)","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"2c0845c7-315a-4b0f-a075-918a2169530c","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:49:23.442Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"156:export async function findAccountByKey(key: string): Promise<AccountKeyRow | null> {\n157-  const row = await db.query.accountKeys.findFirst({\n158-    where: eq(accountKeys.key, key.trim()),\n159-  });\n160-  return row ? credits: number }> {\n29\t  const apiBase = (process.env.NEXTJS_LICENSE_API_URL || DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL).replace(/\\/+$/, '');\n30\t  const validateUrl = `${apiBase}/api/license/validate`;\n31\t  const maskedKey = maskLicenseKey(licenseKey);\n32\t\n33\t  console.log(`[License] Validating ${maskedKey} via ${validateUrl}`);\n34\t\n35\t  try {\n36\t    const response = await fetch(validateUrl, {\n37\t      method: 'POST',\n38\t      headers: {\n39\t        'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n40\t      },\n41\t      body: JSON.stringify({\n42\t        license_key: licenseKey,\n43\t        include_credits: true,\n44\t      }),\n45\t      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS),\n46\t    });\n47\t\n48\t    const responseText = await response.text();\n49\t    let data: { valid?: boolean; credits?: number; error?: string } = {};\n50\t\n51\t    try {\n52\t      data = JSON.parse(responseText);\n53\t    } catch {\n54\t      console.error(`[License] Invalid JSON response for ${maskedKey}: ${responseText.slice(0, 200)}`);\n55\t    }\n56\t\n57\t    const isValid = data.valid === true;\n58\t    const credits = typeof data.credits === 'number' ? data.credits : 0;\n59\t\n60\t    console.log(`[License] ${maskedKey}: status=${response.status}, valid=${isValid}, credits=${credits}${data.error ? `, error=${data.error}` : ''}`);\n61\t\n62\t    // A 5xx (cold start, upstream timeout, internal error) is a transient\n63\t    // failure, not proof the license is invalid — caching it would lock a\n64\t    // paying user out for the full LICENSE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS. Fail this\n65\t    // request closed but leave the cache untouched so the next request\n66\t    // retries the API. A 4xx, by contrast, is a definitive verdict from the\n67\t    // licensing API (revoked/not-found/malformed key → valid:false); those\n68\t    // MUST be cached so repeated requests with the same bad key don't hammer\n69\t    // the licensing API on every call.\n70\t    if (response.status >= 500) {\n71\t      console.warn(`[License] Transient ${response.status} response for ${maskedKey}; not caching`);\n72\t      return { isValid: false, credits: 0 };\n73\t    }\n74\t\n75\t    await cacheLicense(licenseKey, {\n76\t      isValid,\n77\t      credits,\n78\t      cachedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n79\t    });\n80\t\n81\t    return { isValid, credits };\n82\t  } catch (error) {\n83\t    // Network/DNS/timeout failure — we could not reach the license API, so we\n84\t    // cannot conclude the license is invalid. Fail this request\n86\t    // closed; the next request retries against the API.\n87\t    if (error instanceof DOMException && (error.name === 'TimeoutError' || error.name === 'AbortError')) {\n88\t      console.error(`[License] Validation timed out for ${maskedKey} after ${LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS}ms (transient, not cached)`);\n89\t    } else {\n90\t      console.error(`[License] Validation failed for ${maskedKey} (transient, not cached):`, error);\n91\t    }\n92\t    return { isValid: false, credits: 0 };\n93\t  }\n94\t}\n95\t\n96\texport async function validateAuth(input: AuthInput, forceRefresh = false): Promise<AuthResult> {\n97\t  const { licenseKey } = input;\n98\t\n99\t  // HyperWhisper Cloud is licensed-only: a valid license key (which carries the\n100\t  // credit balance) is required for every request. };\n145\t}\n146\t"}]},"uuid":"0311a965-5e05-4421-afc2-0b56db912ffd","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:49:36.734Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/hyperwhisper-cloud/src/middleware/auth.ts","content":"// AUTHENTICATION HELPERS\n// Validates license keys and device trial identifiers\n\nimport { DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL, LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS } from '../lib/constants';\nimport { cacheLicense, getCachedLicense } from '../lib/redis';\nimport { invalidLicenseResponse, licenseRequiredResponse } from '../lib/responses';\n\nexport interface AuthContext {\n  identifier: string; // license key\n  credits: number;\n  licenseKey: string;\n}\n\nexport interface AuthInput {\n  licenseKey?: string;\n}\n\nexport type AuthResult =\n  | { ok: true; value: AuthContext }\n  | { ok: false; response: Response };\n\n// Mask license key for logging (show first 4 and last 4 chars)\nfunction maskLicenseKey(key: string): string {\n  if (key.length <= 8) return '****';\n  return `${key.slice(0, 4)}...${key.slice(-4)}`;\n}\n\nasync function validateLicenseViaApi(licenseKey: string): Promise<{ isValid: boolean; credits: number }> {\n  const apiBase = (process.env.NEXTJS_LICENSE_API_URL || DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL).replace(/\\/+$/, '');\n  const validateUrl = `${apiBase}/api/license/validate`;\n  const maskedKey = maskLicenseKey(licenseKey);\n\n  console.log(`[License] Validating ${maskedKey} via ${validateUrl}`);\n\n  try {\n    const response = await fetch(validateUrl, {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: {\n        'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n      },\n      body: JSON.stringify({\n        license_key: licenseKey,\n        include_credits: true,\n      }),\n      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS),\n    });\n\n    const responseText = await response.text();\n    let data: { valid?: boolean; credits?: number; error?: string } = {};\n\n    try {\n      data = JSON.parse(responseText);\n    } catch {\n      console.error(`[License] Invalid JSON response for ${maskedKey}: ${responseText.slice(0, 200)}`);\n    }\n\n    const isValid = data.valid === true;\n    const credits = typeof data.credits === 'number' ? data.credits : 0;\n\n    console.log(`[License] ${maskedKey}: status=${response.status}, valid=${isValid}, credits=${credits}${data.error ? `, error=${data.error}` : ''}`);\n\n    // A 5xx (cold start, upstream timeout, internal error) is a transient\n    // failure, not proof the license is invalid — caching it would lock a\n    // paying user out for the full LICENSE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS. Fail this\n    // request closed but leave the cache untouched so the next request\n    // retries the API. A 4xx, by contrast, is a definitive verdict from the\n    // licensing API (revoked/not-found/malformed key → valid:false); those\n    // MUST be cached so repeated requests with the same bad key don't hammer\n    // the licensing API on every call.\n    if (response.status >= 500) {\n      console.warn(`[License] Transient ${response.status} response for ${maskedKey}; not caching`);\n      return { isValid: false, credits: 0 };\n    }\n\n    await cacheLicense(licenseKey, {\n      isValid,\n      credits,\n      cachedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    });\n\n    return { isValid, credits };\n  } catch (error) {\n    // Network/DNS/timeout failure — we could not reach the license API, so we\n    // cannot conclude the license is invalid. Do NOT cache: caching invalid\n    // here would lock a paying user out for the full TTL. Fail this request\n    // closed; the next request retries against the API.\n    if (error instanceof DOMException && (error.name === 'TimeoutError' || error.name === 'AbortError')) {\n      console.error(`[License] Validation timed out for ${maskedKey} after ${LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS}ms (transient, not cached)`);\n    } else {\n      console.error(`[License] Validation failed for ${maskedKey} (transient, not cached):`, error);\n    }\n    return { isValid: false, credits: 0 };\n  }\n}\n\nexport async function validateAuth(input: AuthInput, forceRefresh = false): Promise<AuthResult> {\n  const { licenseKey } = input;\n\n  // HyperWhisper Cloud is licensed-only: a valid license key (which carries the\n  // credit balance) is required for every request. There is no anonymous/trial\n  // path — without a key the request is rejected before any provider work.\n  if (!licenseKey) {\n    console.log('[Auth] No license_key provided');\n    return { ok: false, response: licenseRequiredResponse() };\n  }\n\n  const maskedKey = maskLicenseKey(licenseKey);\n\n  if (!forceRefresh) {\n    const cached = await getCachedLicense(licenseKey);\n    if (cached) {\n      console.log(`[Auth] Cache HIT for ${maskedKey}: valid=${cached.isValid}, credits=${cached.credits}, cachedAt=${cached.cachedAt}`);\n      if (!cached.isValid) {\n        return { ok: false, response: invalidLicenseResponse() };\n      }\n      return {\n        ok: true,\n        value: {\n          identifier: licenseKey,\n          credits: cached.credits,\n          licenseKey,\n        },\n      };\n    }\n    console.log(`[Auth] Cache MISS for ${maskedKey}, calling API...`);\n  } else {\n    console.log(`[Auth] Force refresh for ${maskedKey}, bypassing cache...`);\n  }\n\n  const validation = await validateLicenseViaApi(licenseKey);\n  if (!validation.isValid) {\n    console.log(`[Auth] License ${maskedKey} is INVALID`);\n    return { ok: false, response: invalidLicenseResponse() };\n  }\n\n  console.log(`[Auth] License ${maskedKey} is VALID with ${validation.credits} credits`);\n  return {\n    ok: true,\n    value: {\n      identifier: licenseKey,\n      credits: validation.credits,\n      licenseKey,\n    },\n  };\n}\n","numLines":146,"startLine":1,"totalLines":146}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"70cfb073-7331-4c15-86c0-747476bfdcb9","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

Verifies the license status is \"granted\" (not revoked/disabled)\n */\nexport async function checkLicenseKey(\n  licenseKey: string\n): Promise<LicenseCheckResult> {\n  // Query database for the license\n  let license = await findAccountByKey(licenseKey.trim());\n\n  // POLAR FALLBACK: If not found in DB, try Polar API\n  if (!license) {\n    const polarImport = await importLicenseFromPolar(licenseKey.trim());\n\n    if (!polarImport.success) {\n      return {\n        valid: false,\n        error: polarImport.error || \"License key not found\",\n        status: 400,\n      };\n    }\n\n    // Re-query the newly imported license\n    license = await findAccountById(polarImport.licenseId!);\n\n    if (!license) {\n      return {\n        valid: false,\n        error: \"Failed to retrieve imported license\",\n        status: 500,\n      };\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Check status\n  if (license.status !== \"granted\") {\n    return {\n      valid: false,\n      error: `License is ${license.status}`,\n      status: 400,\n    };\n  }\n\n  return { valid: true, license };\n}","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"811865d1-0c40-4655-83ad-8c5ddf35b61a","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:49:38.602Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":" * 3. Verifies the license status is \"granted\" (not revoked/disabled)\n */\nexport async function checkLicenseKey(\n  licenseKey: string\n): Promise<LicenseCheckResult> {\n  // Query database for the license\n  let license = await findAccountByKey(licenseKey.trim());\n\n  // POLAR FALLBACK: If not found in DB, try Polar API\n  if (!license) {\n    const polarImport = await importLicenseFromPolar(licenseKey.trim());\n\n    if (!polarImport.success) {\n      return {\n        valid: false,\n        error: polarImport.error || \"License key not found\",\n        status: 400,\n      };\n    }\n\n    // Re-query the newly imported license\n    license = await findAccountById(polarImport.licenseId!);\n\n    if (!license) {\n      return {\n        valid: false,\n        error: \"Failed to retrieve imported license\",\n        status: 500,\n      };\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Check status\n  if (license.status !== \"granted\") {\n    return {\n      valid: false,\n      error: `License is ${license.status}`,\n      status: 400,\n    };\n  }\n\n  return { valid: true, license };\n}","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"971cdefb-6fef-4d08-95e6-aedead37e895","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"2deff211-96d3-4829-b38f-a6594134457c","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a90e691c4f3fd6396","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdWJMruSXGamb22V8oVZT","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Both candidates judged. Findings below.\n\n---\n\n## `[hyperwhisper-cloud/src/middleware/credits.ts:23]` — **CONFIRMED** (with a severity correction)\n\n**The claim is exactly right, and it is violated by HyperWhisper's own shipped macOS client — no attacker needed.**\n\nReservation side (`credits.ts:22-25` → `transcribe.ts:139,180-182`):\n```ts\nconst estimatedMinutes = sizeBytes / BYTES_PER_MINUTE_ESTIMATE;   // 480_000\n...\nconst estimatedCostUsd = (estimatedSeconds / 60) * usdPerMinute + promptReservationUsd;\n```\n`constants.ts:11`: `export const BYTES_PER_MINUTE_ESTIMATE = 480_000; // 64kbps encoded audio`\n\nBilling side (`cost-calculator.ts:184-191`) uses the **provider-reported** duration:\n```ts\nconst durationMinutes = durationSeconds / 60;\nconst base = durationMinutes * ELEVENLABS_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE;\n```\nSame rate constant on both sides, so the reservation/deduction ratio is exactly `clientBitrate / 64 kbps`. There is no post-transcription re-check: `transcribe.ts:679` calls `deductCredits(authResult.value, result.costUsd, …)` unconditionally.\n\n**Trigger path (fully in-repo, first-party):**\n`app/macos/.../RecordingLifecycle.swift:678-690` — when the 16 kHz mono WAV exceeds 25 MB (~13 min of speech) the client converts to M4A and uploads that. `app/macos/.../AudioFileConverter.swift:171-174`:\n```swift\nlet bitrate: Int\nif channels <= 1 {\n    bitrate = 32_000\n```\n**32 kbps mono — exactly half the assumed floor.** So every macOS recording over ~13 minutes under-reserves 2x.\n\nConcrete instance: 20 min of speech → M4A ≈ 4.8 MB. `POST /transcribe`, `X-STT-Provider: elevenlabs`, `Content-Length: 4800000`.\n`transcribeRoute` → `estimateCreditsForProviderFallbacks(4.8e6,'elevenlabs')` → `estimateAudioSecondsFromSize` = 600 s → 10 × $0.00983 = **98.3 credits reserved**. ElevenLabs returns `duration: 1200` → `computeElevenLabsTranscriptionCost(1200)` = **196.6 credits deducted**. I1 violated 2:1. (Note this is worst on the *self-only* chains — `elevenlabs`, `assemblyai`, `openai`, `gemini`, `mistral`, `soniox`, `azure-mai`, `google-chirp` — where the `Math.max(...rates)` chain headroom doesn't accidentally absorb it; a `deepgram`-primary request reserves at the ElevenLabs rate and so has ~1.78x headroom that nearly cancels a 2x bitrate error.)\n\n**Severity correction — this is the bounded, self-correcting variety, not unbounded spend.** `deductCreditBalance` → `spendCreditGrantsByProvenance` (`nextjs/src/lib/db-layer.ts:585-591`) deducts `Math.min(grantRemaining, remainingToDeduct)` per grant, so the balance **floors at 0, never goes negative** — the excess is absorbed by the business rather than carried forward. But `recordLicenseUsage` writes `credits_remaining` back into the Redis cache on success (`credits.ts:85-91`), so the *next* request sees 0 and `validateCredits` 402s it. Net exposure is **one request, capped at roughly `(bitrateRatio − 1) × remaining balance`**, and only in the narrow window where the balance sits between the reservation and the true cost — a well-funded user is simply charged the correct amount. At 32 kbps that ceiling is ~1x the balance; the candidate's 12 kbps Opus figure (~4.3x) I could **not** verify, since which sub-32 kbps codecs the STT providers actually accept and meter is outside the tree.\n\n**Intent check (this is the closest call):** the 64 kbps assumption is undeniably deliberate — `credits.ts:11` \"Minimum encoded bitrate expected from clients for conservative credit estimation\", the constant's own comment, and `transcribe.test.ts:19-25` `test('does not under-estimate one minute of 64kbps audio')`. But that test pins only the ≥64 kbps direction; nothing anywhere documents or tests under-reservation below it as an accepted outcome, and the vendor's own encoder falsifies the stated \"minimum expected\" by 2x. So: the *assumption* is intentional, the *violation of it by the shipped client* is not. Not downgraded.\n\n---\n\n## `[hyperwhisper-cloud/src/middleware/credits.ts:74]` — **CONFIRMED**\n\n**The swallow is an oversight, not best-effort billing, and it is unrecoverable.** I checked all four things you asked:\n\n**1. Is a non-2xx a designed path (e.g. insufficient balance)?** No. `nextjs/app/api/license/credits/route.ts` POST returns non-2xx only for: missing/invalid `license_key` (400), invalid `amount` (400), **key not found (400)**, `status !== \"granted\"` (400), a DB failure (409, body literally `\"Failed to deduct credits. Please retry.\"`), or an unhandled throw (500). Insufficient balance is *not* among them — the SQL spend floors at 0 and returns **200**. So every non-2xx is a genuinely lost charge, and the 409 explicitly asks for a retry the cloud never performs.\n\n**2. Any retry / DLQ / reconciliation?** None. `grep -riE \"reconcil|dead.?letter|dlq|retry_queue|pending_deduction|unbilled\"` across `hyperwhisper-cloud/` and `nextjs/` (excluding `node_modules`) returns nothing on this path.\n\n**3. Does `performDeduction` resolve successfully after the drop?** Yes — `return;` at line 81 exits `recordLicenseUsage` as a normal `Promise<void>` resolution, so:\n```ts\nawait recordLicenseUsage(auth.identifier, creditsUsed, metadata);\nreturn creditsUsed;   // line 145 — reports a positive charge that never happened\n```\n`deductCredits` resolves, `inFlightDeductions` clears, and every call site (`transcribe.ts:679 .catch(console.error)`, `post-process.ts:256/280`, `assistant.ts:352`, `ws-streaming-deepgram.ts:209`) sees success. The SIGTERM drain (`index.ts:85-99`) awaits a promise that charged nothing.\n\n**4. Tests?** No `credits.test.ts` exists. Every test that mocks the endpoint (`transcribe-multi-provider.test.ts`, 22 sites) returns `Response.json({ credits_remaining: 999 })` — the non-2xx branch has **zero** coverage. And the neighbouring comment at `credits.ts:99-103` states the *opposite* intent: *\"otherwise the user keeps the transcript but the charge is silently dropped.\"* The author built a whole SIGTERM drain to prevent exactly this, 20 lines below the code that does it anyway.\n\n**Deterministic trigger constructible from code alone** (stronger than the candidate's \"Vercel 5xx\" framing, which needs external state):\n\n`validateAuth` (`auth.ts:119,140`) sets `identifier: licenseKey` — the **raw client-supplied key**, never trimmed or case-normalized. Now:\n\n- `POST /transcribe?license_key=abcd-EFGH` where the stored key is `ABCD-EFGH` (a Polar-era key entered with different casing).\n- `auth.ts:36` → `POST /api/license/validate` → `checkLicenseKey` → `findAccountByKey(key.trim())` misses (`db-layer.ts:158` is `eq(accountKeys.key, …)` — case-sensitive) → Polar fallback. `license-validation.ts:43-51` documents this precisely:\n  > *\"findAccountByKey is a case-sensitive exact match, so a different casing or whitespace variant of an already-imported key misses the DB lookup and reaches this fallback.\"*\n\n  The dedupe returns the **canonical** row, `checkLicenseKey` returns `{valid: true}` with the real balance, and the cloud caches `license:abcd-EFGH` as valid.\n- Every subsequent request passes preflight and runs the provider.\n- Then `recordLicenseUsage('abcd-EFGH', …)` → `POST /api/license/credits` → `findAccountByKey('abcd-EFGH')` → **null** → `400 \"License key not found\"` → line 74-81 swallows it.\n\nBecause line 86's `cacheLicense` never runs, the cached balance also never decrements — this does **not** self-correct after one request the way the `:23` bug does. That key gets **unlimited free transcription indefinitely**.
